## 2026-07-01 — #370 rig-restore-watchdog: distinct partial alert + rate-limit (PR TBD, v1.7.0-dev.179)
- **Ticket-validator**: STILL_VALID — `rig-restore-watchdog.sh` lacked classification of partial vs positive restores and fired Discord unconditionally on every ~2-min pass while OBS was unreadable.
- **Part A** — `rig_classify_restore(act, obs_unreadable, obs_failed)` → `kind=positive|partial` in `scripts/lib/rig-restore-decision.sh`. Positive = full restore (0 unreadable, 0 failed). Partial = marker KEPT.
- **Part B** — `rig_alert_throttle(kind, current_sig, prior_sig, prior_passes)` → `alert_now=0|1 + new_sig + new_passes`. Positive always alerts. Partial throttled to once per N=5 passes (or signature change). State file extended to key=value with `confirm + alert_sig + alert_passes`. Two-write pattern: early (crash-safe confirm) + late (alert state). Backward-compat with legacy bare-number state.
- **Part C** — 13 new tests in `tests/harness_rig_restore_watchdog.rs`. 60/60 pass. No existing test weakened.
- **TDD**: RED `7d1527308` → GREEN `8ccf3ef7d`. All Tier-0 checks clean.
- **No live deploy** — watchdog ships DISABLED; supervisor enables separately.

## 2026-07-01 — #369 auto-grow root + 512M /var/cache in builders + fleet disk-space guard (PR #384, v1.7.0-dev.178)
- **Ticket-validator**: PARTIAL — setup.sh + setup-device.sh already had 512M /var/cache; image builders (create-usb-linux.sh + build-image.sh) were MISSING it + missing grow-root + missing cloud-guest-utils.
- **Root cause**: cam4 shipped 3.5G/92%-full on a 57G disk (partition never grown); cam1 /var/cache was 100M/100%-full (old image pre-#306). Both image builders lacked auto-grow AND the uniform 512M /var/cache fstab line.
- **Part 1 — auto-grow**: New `scripts/lib/camera-box-grow-root.sh` (canonical helper, installed by both builders at `/usr/local/sbin/`). `systemd/camera-box-grow-root.service` (oneshot, `ConditionPathExists=!/var/lib/camera-box/grow-root.done`, `After=local-fs.target`, `Before=multi-user.target`). Fault-tolerant: exits 0 when root is not the last partition (3-partition overlay image layout). Both builders install + enable it.
- **Part 2 — 512M /var/cache**: Added `tmpfs /var/cache tmpfs defaults,...,size=512M` to fstab heredoc in both builders.
- **Part 3 — cloud-guest-utils**: Added to `apt-get install -y` in both builders (provides growpart).
- **Part 4 — fleet disk-space guard (dev1, SHIPS DISABLED)**: `scripts/cam-disk-guard.sh` SSHes CAM1/CAM2/CAM4, checks df /, df /var/cache, fires Discord alert at ≥80%. `scripts/lib/disk-guard-thresholds.sh` is the pure threshold seam (unit-tested). `systemd/cam-disk-guard.{service,timer}` ship DISABLED (same pattern as rig-restore-watchdog #281 Fix#3).
- **TDD** (content-assertion gate): version bump `2060cae36` → RED `bc7a0a088` (4 new assertions in `tests/appliance_boot_hardening.rs`: tests 13-16) → GREEN `ea771fef9` (implementation) → refactor `6ea08a216` (extract helper to shared file, add Before= per reviewer).
- **Key decisions**: grow-root helper extracted to `scripts/lib/camera-box-grow-root.sh` (code-review follow-up; was duplicated verbatim in both builders via heredoc, now shared via `install`). `Before=multi-user.target` added to service unit (explicit ordering). cam3 excluded from fleet guard (pending re-image, #301).
- **Merge**: PR #384 merged `d457746b0ec11d80fac35844fcb404a49d3bf4f2`, main CI all green. No deploy (provisioning-only change; next image built from CI artifact picks up the new service). Closes #369.

## 2026-07-01 — #356 residual cam1 over-count: cross-recording reconciliation (PR #383, v1.7.0-dev.177)
- **Scope (rescoped by owner)**: #356's dominant strih/cam2 20080 over-count was already fixed by #360/PR #361; the OWNER rescoped #356 to the RESIDUAL cam1 over-count (~2251 on 354001). Fixed ONLY that; did NOT touch the #360 strih gap-ignore or `src/probe/burn_contiguity.rs` (#216/#226/#267 untouched).
- **Root cause**: cam1 contiguity is read from the CLEAN strih recording (#133); at the 60→30 hop with ~450ms latency the small cam1 burn QR softens → some cam1 ids UNREADABLE in the strih recording though the frame WAS delivered (present downstream) → wrongly counted REAL DROP, inflating the merge headline.
- **Fix**: new pure Tier-0 kernel `src/burn_reconcile.rs::cam1_real_drops_proven_delivered_downstream` = `real_drops ∩ downstream_cam1_ids`. Wired in the shared verdict loop of `src/bin/recording-verdict.rs` (cam1 node only, gated `strih_data.is_some()`): `burn_ids_in(stream_frames, burn_cam1_run_id)` → downgrade matching cam1 `ClassifiedMissing` from `RealDrop`→`BurnUnreadable`.
- **STRICT-TEST SAFETY**: only moves the CLASSIFICATION bucket; never touches `missing_ids`, so `is_contiguous()`/`is_zero()` are unchanged → a downgraded id still makes the node NOT-zero → NO false ZERO possible (verified by adversarial safety review). An id absent from the stream recording (genuine loss OR 30fps-decimated) is NEVER downgraded — stays REAL DROP.
- **TDD** (bug fix, RED→GREEN): version bump `c96707b31` → RED `c8cb21c5f` → GREEN `f50f194e5`. Pure locks: `real_drop_present_downstream_is_flagged_for_burn_unreadable`, `only_the_present_subset_is_flagged`, SAFETY `real_drop_absent_downstream_stays_real_drop` (burn_reconcile.rs). End-to-end (probe-gated): `cam1_real_drop_present_downstream_is_burn_unreadable_not_real_drop_356` + SAFETY `cam1_real_drop_absent_from_both_stays_real_drop_356` (recording-verdict.rs). Fixed `merge_of_partials_reproduces_the_fused_verdict` drop-case (stream gap None→Some(5) so the real drop is absent from BOTH recordings, still a genuine drop under reconciliation).
- **Local verify**: pure tests RED→GREEN observed on default features (`cargo test --lib burn_reconcile # airuleset:build-ok`); fmt/check/clippy default-features clean. Probe-gated E2E + fixed equivalence test verified on CI (Test job green). Did NOT compile `--features probe` locally (Tier-0 policy).
- **Merge**: PR #383 merged `303d076760a91170237e674f49b30974e59144e1`, main CI all green. No deploy (recording-verdict is a CI-built probe artifact; no web dashboard). Closes #356.

## 2026-06-30 (cont.) — #373 + #374 (bundled, joined PR #378, NO rig, v1.7.0-dev.170)
- **Version bump**: `e61f1c410` (dev.169→dev.170), FIRST commit. Same `dev` / PR #378 (the #364 colour-gate PR — these commits join it).
- **#373 — E2E STRICT GATE: optical-span collapse can vacuously PASS.** The recording-verdict headline proved DELIVERY (per-node burn contiguity) + the #363 optical read + #364 colour, but never how LONG the analyzed optical span was. When the cam2 optical read COLLAPSES (green-cast camera: dies partway, only a small early cluster, or never decodes), the span shrinks to a handful of frames; over it `optical_undecodable==0` and the burn window is trivially contiguous, so `is_zero()` alone vacuously PASSED the headline (live repro: `nodes.strih.analyzed_secs=0.0`, `frames=0`, yet `overall_pass=true`). Fix: new pure Tier-0 crate-root module `src/recording_span_gate.rs` (`span_secs(frames,fps)` + `analyzed_span_long_enough(secs,min_secs)` = `secs>=min_secs`). `NodeVerdict` carries `optical_span_frames`; the #186 headline ANDs `is_zero()` with `nv.span_ok(capture_fps,min_secs)` (and `all_zero` likewise). `is_zero()` UNCHANGED (still the per-node delivery+optical+colour gate, matched to the fixtures); the duration FLOOR is a run-level headline term. Per-node JSON now carries `analyzed_secs`/`span_ok`/`min_secs`. **Reconciliation** (e2e skill says the analyzed span is "DIAGNOSTIC only — does NOT gate the headline"): that guards against FAILING a real full-length run for a short diagnostic NOTE; #373 is the opposite — a COLLAPSED span vacuously PASSING. A duration FLOOR rejects only the collapsed/partial read; it never fails a real `>=min_secs` run (test: 9000-frame/300 s run still passes). **RED→GREEN** (Tier-0, observed `cargo test --lib recording_span_gate # airuleset:build-ok`): `test:[red]` `d20e1bfbe` — `collapsed_optical_span_must_fail_the_headline_373` + `partial_early_cluster_below_floor_must_fail_the_headline_373` FAIL with the vacuous stub (returns true); `fix:[green]` `12f339a08` — real `>=min_secs` floor ⇒ all 4 pure tests GREEN. Probe-gated binary locks (CI): `collapsed_optical_span_fails_the_headline_duration_gate_373`, `empty_optical_span_has_zero_frames_and_fails_the_duration_gate_373`. (Closes #373)
- **#374 — recording-verdict 2 minor nits** (refactor `78ea28191`, Closes #374). nit 1: strih+stream read the SAME stream recording, so each `node_verdict` recomputed the optical-span scan — now `OpticalSpanFacts` + `optical_span_facts()` derive undecodable count + span frames from ONE `optical_span`; the headline computes facts ONCE per source (`stream_optical` shared by strih+stream, `cam1_optical` for cam1) and passes them to `node_verdict_with_optical`. A `mod tests` `node_verdict` helper keeps the 13 existing call sites unchanged (and avoids a dead-code wrapper in the non-test bin build). nit 2: extracted the pure `node_verdict_lines()` (unit-testable) + an `explained` guard so an empty burn window WITH an interior optical hole no longer co-prints BOTH the OPTICAL-UNDECODABLE line AND the generic "NO burn id decoded" line; the sole-reason no-burn line still prints. Tests `print_node_verdict_does_not_co_print_no_burn_with_optical_374` + `…keeps_no_burn_line_when_it_is_the_sole_reason_374`.
- **Tier-0 discipline**: the gate DECISION is the pure default-features `recording_span_gate` (RED→GREEN observed locally). `bin/recording-verdict` is `required-features=["probe"]` → compiled ON CI ONLY; binary edits verified by careful review + the probe-gated locks (the SUPERVISOR owns CI for PR #378). Local cheap checks green: fmt/check/clippy/test --no-run + 247 lib tests.

## 2026-06-30 — #362 + #353 (bundled clean-code PR, NO rig, v1.7.0-dev.164)
- **Version bump**: `7efad0c26` (dev.163→dev.164), FIRST commit.
- **#362** — fresh cam-box image missing NDI/audio runtime deps (a fresh CAM3 USB clone for the #301 re-image booted but camera-box crash-looped: libndi could not dlopen — missing ALSA + avahi libs, `/usr/lib/ndi` not on the linker path, no avahi-daemon for mDNS NDI discovery). Baked the deps into ALL THREE builders: `create-usb-linux.sh` (base image — had NONE), `setup.sh` (+libavahi-common3 +avahi-utils +ld.so.conf/ndi.conf+ldconfig), `setup-device.sh` (+libavahi-common3 +avahi-utils +libasound2t64). Each now installs `libasound2t64 libavahi-client3 libavahi-common3 avahi-daemon avahi-utils`, writes `/etc/ld.so.conf.d/ndi.conf`=`/usr/lib/ndi` (+ldconfig), and `systemctl enable avahi-daemon`. avahi-daemon is mDNS only — no DanteSync clock conflict (cam4 runs both). RED `6298057ef` (3 content-assertion guards in `tests/appliance_boot_hardening.rs`, #307 pattern) → GREEN `3dbdfdebd`; clippy doc fix `061f8ddb7`. Build-time scripts verified by content-assertion in CI — NOT a rig deploy.
- **#353** — rig-restore-watchdog: replaced fragile OBS scene-name scraping with a MARKER file. `rig-heartbeat.sh` gained `rig_e2e_marker_path/set/clear/present`; the marker is written on harness entry and removed ONLY on clean exit (an unclean SIGKILL/interrupted-trap death leaves it → durable stranded signal, distinct from the heartbeat which the refresher self-removes on death). `rig-restore-decision.sh` consumes `RIG_E2E_MARKER`: when present (heartbeat already gated by Rule 1) restore EVERY observed OBS box regardless of scene; scene-list kept as marker-less fallback; cam restore unchanged (reliable down/probe). Dropped now-legacy `REC-STRIH-TMP` from the DEFAULT `RIG_KNOWN_TEST_SCENES` in BOTH decision (`…:-PHASE2-PROBE`) + watchdog (per #343: stream box now records prod scene PRO). Watchdog reads the marker→exports it→clears it after restore (so a stale marker can't re-trigger/re-alert). `recording-e2e.sh` sets the marker next to `rig_heartbeat_start`, clears it in `cleanup()`. RED `e069c069f` (9 RED: marker decision/helpers/wiring + rewritten `rec_strih_tmp_no_longer_default_scene_marker_detects_instead`) → GREEN `d36ea6593`. Heartbeat gate left intact.
- **Review fixes (PR #368, /code-review parallel agents)** — RED `dc356f3f3` → GREEN `5f2f8bed7` + test-hardening `d8cdd08db`:
  - **#353 masking bug (CONFIRMED)**: the watchdog cleared the marker on ANY act (gated only on marker presence). If OBS was unreadable that pass (or a teardown failed) while a cam was down, the marker was cleared though the OBS box stayed stranded on a custom/env scene → never detected again. Fix: pure `rig_marker_should_clear <marker> <act> <obs_unreadable> <obs_failed>` — clear ONLY after a positive full OBS restore; `restore_obs` now returns its real teardown rc; main() counts unreadable OBS boxes + failed teardowns.
  - **#353 finding 3**: `rig_e2e_marker_set` swallowed its write rc (dead `|| echo WARNING` guard) → now returns the real rc; marker path co-located with the heartbeat's writability-checked dir (was `${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run}` — `/run` fails for a non-root write).
  - **#353 finding 2** (stale marker tears down healthy-prod OBS): the owner's deliberate marker-keys-regardless-of-scene tradeoff — mitigated by ships-disabled+supervisor-verify, heartbeat gate, 2-confirm, reboot-volatile marker, and the new clear-after-full-restore (one idempotent prod-reassert then clears). NOT re-introducing scene-dependence (would defeat #353).
  - **#362 test hardening**: NDI-deps content assertions matched comment/echo lines in setup-device.sh (not revert-proof for that builder) → `appears_on_active_line()` requires a real command line.
- **Local verification** (Tier-0, default features): `appliance_boot_hardening` 13/13, `harness_rig_restore_watchdog` 46/46; dependent harness suites (recording_e2e_*, with_rig_restore, rig_test_dropin_cleanup, clock_offset_painter_gate, version_integrity_gate) all green; `cargo fmt/check/clippy --all-targets -D warnings` clean. NO rig touched, NO probe build.


## 2026-06-29 — #338 capture-colour regression: stop forcing hue=0 (pink) + auto-desaturating grab (#312 read) (worker, v1.7.0-dev.153)
- **Problem (confirmed by supervisor with live before/after pixels):** `src/capture.rs` `color_production_controls()` forced `hue=0`, doc-claimed "neutral" — but the ShadowCast V4L2 hue is `min=0 max=100 default=50`, so `hue=0` is a MAX shift = a PINK tint (#338: one cam pink, the NZXT cam — no controls → device defaults → clean). All boxes are fed from ONE source via an HDMI splitter, so the tint is purely the forced control. Separately, `certified_cam1_controls()` (sharp set `saturation=0`/`contrast=75`) was auto-selected for grab/QR runs (`select_capture_controls(None, record_grab=true)`) and HURT decode (#312 run 312005: CAM1 ShadowCast ~50% undecodable vs CAM4/NZXT on defaults reading the same monitor clean).
- **VALIDATED still real (clean-code):** current `color_production_controls()` had the `V4L2_CID_HUE value:0` entry; `select_capture_controls(None,true)` returned `certified_cam1_controls()` (saturation=0). The two RED tests panicked against the unfixed tree, proving the buggy code present.
- **RED `d98018c77` → GREEN `715a52276`** (version bump `6de8b6caa` first). RED added `production_controls_must_not_force_hue_338` + `grab_selection_must_not_desaturate_312`. GREEN: dropped the HUE entry from the colour set (now saturation=50+contrast=50 = device defaults, keeps the #296 saturation self-heal); dropped the `None if record_grab => certified_cam1_controls()` arm so grab+production both pick the colour set; `record_grab` → `_record_grab`; `certified_cam1_controls()` + the `certified` env keyword stay opt-in via `CAMERA_BOX_CAPTURE_CONTROLS=certified`. Updated 4 apply-path tally tests (2-control set: NZXT failed 2, ShadowCast applied 2, partial-support uses contrast as the missing control, clamp adjusted 2) + the 3 selection/value tests (renamed `_no_hue_338`, `_uses_color_set_not_sharp_312`; production-path no-hue assert) — each commented that the old assertion encoded the regression.
- **scripts/recording-e2e.sh:** the two `v4l2-ctl --set-ctrl` preflight/launch lines → `saturation=50,contrast=50` (device defaults); `[0/8]`/comment text updated to cite #338/#312.
- **Scope:** Closes #338 only. Addresses the CAM1 read part of #312 but #312 stays OPEN (the all-cambox zero-loss read is the supervisor's rig validation). RIG NOT touched (clean-code only); supervisor deploys `camera-box-linux-amd64` + verifies pink-gone + decode-clean on the rig.
- **Pre-existing (NOT mine, NOT CI-gated):** `ruff check .` flags 38 errors, all in untouched files (mostly vendored `vendor/obs-studio/` Python + pre-existing `scripts/recording-e2e-report.py`); CI runs no ruff/flake8/pylint. My diff (capture.rs + recording-e2e.sh) adds zero.
- **Playbook:** updated `.claude/skills/capture/SKILL.md` — corrected the COLOUR/SHARP table (colour now default for production AND grab; sharp opt-in only), added the #338 "hue=0 is NOT neutral / never force hue" + #312 "sharp set not auto-applied to grab" gotchas, fixed the 3×→2× NZXT warn count.

## 2026-06-28 — #289 cam-box grab realtime isolation (worker, v1.7.0-dev.133)
- **Problem:** `isolcpus=3` reserved core 3 on every box but NOTHING pinned to it — the SCHED_FIFO capture/emit ran on the loaded general cores (0-2: USB kworkers, ssh, painter), so it wobbled under box load (CAM2 55-60fps + underruns + 28ms head_skew); core 3 idle. The in-binary affinity was pinning to core **1** (`main.rs apply_cpu_affinity`), not the isolated core.
- **VALIDATED still real:** current `main.rs:104` pinned capture to core 1, `intercom.rs` to core 1, `ndi_display.rs` to core 0; systemd unit had NO `CPUAffinity=`. So the grab was NOT on the isolcpus core. Confirmed against current code (the partial-fix comment on #289 had only applied a runtime taskset + a `CPUAffinity=3` drop-in by hand, not in-tree).
- **RED `e1837064e` → GREEN `96fbb201e`** (version bump `66cb5b1a6` first). New `src/affinity.rs` — pure, unit-tested: `parse_cpulist`, `select_capture_core` (derive isolcpus core from /sys robustly; highest online isolated; fallback last online; `CAMERA_BOX_CAPTURE_CORE` override), `select_painter_cores` (online minus capture core), `parse_capture_irqs` (generic xhci/ehci/ohci/uvcvideo discovery from /proc/interrupts), `smp_affinity_mask_hex`. Glue (sched_setaffinity / /sys / /proc-irq IO) is thin.
- **Wiring:** capture+emit thread → isolated core (`main.rs pin_capture_thread`); painter (`probe/painter.rs`), `--display` (`ndi_display.rs`), intercom (`intercom.rs`) → OFF it via `pin_off_capture_core`. New `--setup-irq-affinity` flag runs `setup_irq_affinity` (route USB capture IRQ→isolated core, idempotent, best-effort, managed-MSI rejects logged non-fatal). systemd unit: `ExecStartPre=-...--setup-irq-affinity` + `CPUAffinity=3` (soft belt-and-braces; binary refines aux threads off it; binary's /sys derivation is authoritative).
- **CI:** coverage `--ignore-filename-regex` adds `src/affinity.rs` (syscall/IO glue, kms.rs precedent — pure fns still unit-tested); mutants `--exclude-re` the 7 glue fns, pure selection/parse fns stay mutated (added 3 edge tests: isolated-not-online, duplicate-cores idempotent, out-of-range skip; dropped a redundant empty-guard that would survive mutation).
- **OUT OF SCOPE / deferred:** kernel-cmdline tuning (`nohz_full=3`/`rcu_nocbs=3`/`irqaffinity=`) needs the #295 safe-grub (ro/synced root) work first (grub edits bricked 2 boxes, #295/#301) → filed **#303**. DO NOT edit grub.
- **RIG: NOT touched** (no ssh/MCP to cam boxes). Deliverable = clean-code PR. The steady-60fps-under-load measurement at strih is the SUPERVISOR's rig step. PR dev→main, auto-merge.

## 2026-06-27 — #11 mixed 60/30 decimation-aware verdict + per-box fps (worker, v1.7.0-dev.127)
- Advances #11 (final mixed topology cam60→strih60→stream30); does NOT close it (the terminal mixed E2E does). NOT merged (supervisor re-reviews the no-masking decimation logic + merges + does the rig config + the mixed E2E).
- **VALIDATED still real:** RED proof. `in_window_decimation_step2_extra_missing_id_is_a_real_drop_not_masked` (+ two-lost-frames variant) FAILED on the pre-fix tree (`740b51c0e`): the PerRenderTick forward gap was UNCONDITIONALLY ignored, so a gap of 4 (where 2 is by-design decimation) read as a false ZERO — real strih→stream loss masked. Bug confirmed against current code.
- **§A decimation-aware verdict (the load-bearing fix):** RED `740b51c0e` → GREEN `6155f7356`. `burn_contiguity_in_window_with_step(expected_step)` in `src/probe/burn_contiguity.rs:~380` — for a PerRenderTick node with `expected_step >= 2`, a forward gap == step is the by-design decimation (not loss); gap > step charges the excess `gap/expected_step − 1` (integer; jitter of step±1 charges 0) as `RealDrop`. `expected_step == 1` = today's unconditional-ignore (back-compat wrapper `burn_contiguity_in_window` passes 1). `recording-verdict.rs` (`build_and_print_verdict` NodeSpec.step + `node_render_step` + `extract_partial_flagged_frames`): strih burn=2 (read from 30fps stream rec), stream burn=1 (own OBS), cam1 set-based. New rig-pinned args `--strih-emit-fps`(60)/`--stream-capture-fps`(30) DECOUPLE the loss step from the diagnostic `--capture-fps` (so the merge keeps `--capture-fps 60` for cam1's diagnostic while strih step stays 2). RED→GREEN tests: decimation passes; decimation+real-drop FAILS (no masking); step=1 still catches a real drop; `node_render_step` topology map. cam1→strih leg UNCHANGED (PerEmittedFrame set-based catches every real 60fps drop).
- **§B per-box config:** `vendor/README.md` output_fps split host-keyed (strih=60/stream=30); `drift-guard.sh` resolves the pin from `host=` (FAILS loudly on unknown/empty host) + check_pins validates both; `recording-e2e.sh` STRIH_CAPTURE_FPS=60/STREAM_CAPTURE_FPS=30 + threads the pinned decimation step; `camera-set.sh` GENLOCK_FPS default 30→60 (cam1 emits 60; deploy-fleet sources it). Tests: drift_guard, version_integrity_gate (STREAM_PINNED→30), harness_recording_e2e_paths, harness_genlock_sender_env updated.
- **Genlock 60→30 decimator (§3):** no change — already supported (`genlock_decide` STEADY consumes one queued frame per 30fps render tick; preload=1 absorbs jitter; `tests/genlock_preload.rs`).
- All on PR (one) #<TBD>. Local Tier-0 green (fmt/check/clippy/test --no-run); probe tests green via build-ok bypass (40 burn_contiguity + 47 recording-verdict inline + 12 recording_verdict integration).

## 2026-06-25 — #124 single canonical OBS plugin-load path (worker, v1.7.0-dev.105)
- **VALIDATED PARTIAL (ticket-validator + own live read, win-strih/win-stream-snv MCP):** the DLL ambiguity the issue describes does NOT exist on the live rig TODAY. Both boxes carry EXACTLY ONE `distroav.dll`, in `C:\ProgramData\obs-studio\plugins\distroav\bin\64bit\` (663040 B; strih 06-23 19:43, stream 06-23 19:45), loaded by the Program Files genlock `obs64.exe` (strih PID 6244, stream PID 10844), render tick ENABLED, DistroAV 6.2.1 / NDI 6.3.2.0. **NONE** under `Program Files\obs-studio\obs-plugins\64bit` (only first-party plugins there; the `data\obs-plugins\distroav` folder is resources/locale, no .dll). `%APPDATA%\obs-studio\plugins` does not exist; not portable. So the issue's premise was INVERTED — canonical = ProgramData, not Program Files\obs-plugins. **Nothing to clean on the boxes.**
- **The real gap (still open):** drift-guard checked versions/fps/genlock/latency but had ZERO plugin-path check — a future deploy could still drop a 2nd `distroav.dll` into Program Files\obs-plugins and recreate the shadow (the #119 mixed-version class). So #124 = add the ENFORCEMENT + doc the invariant, NOT clean the boxes.
- **Implemented (drift-guard a repo change → PR):**
  - `vendor/README.md`: new pinned `canonical_plugin_path` row = `C:\ProgramData\obs-studio\plugins\distroav\bin\64bit` + the no-shadow invariant.
  - `scripts/drift-guard.sh`: `drift_check_plugin_paths` pure fn + `--compare` key `distroav_dll_paths`. EXACTLY ONE `distroav.dll` across the OBS scan paths, at canonical → OK; >1 (shadow) or lone-off-canonical → DRIFT (exit 20); empty → UNKNOWN (exit 11, never silent clean). Wired into check_pins + compare_observed + usage. Case-insensitive dir compare; entry may be dir or full .dll path.
  - `/drift-guard` command: step 1c enumerates every `distroav.dll` across the 3 scan paths → feeds `distroav_dll_paths`.
  - genlock + obs-ops skills: canonical-path invariant + no-shadow rule documented.
- **RED→GREEN:** `tests/drift_guard.rs::{drift_check_plugin_paths_pure_flags_shadow_and_wrong_path, compare_fails_loudly_on_plugin_shadow, compare_plugin_paths_unknown_when_not_read}` — RED on `2b665f996` (fn+key absent → RC=127 / ignored-key → no drift), GREEN on `419540b77`. All 18 drift_guard tests pass.
- **Live end-to-end proof:** ran the engine with the REAL strih observed paths → NO DRIFT (exit 0); simulated a Program Files shadow → DRIFT DETECTED (exit 20). Negative + positive both proven against real data.
- **GOTCHA (pre-push hook false positive):** the inherited `ec1fd65a9 fix(purge-target): #185` (script-only, already `[no-test:]`-bypassed on its own dev push, not yet on main) is the FIRST commit in the dev→main delta, so pre-push-test-check Gate-2 ("bugfix before any test commit") re-flagged it on this PR. My own #124 work is correctly RED→GREEN ordered. Resolved with an honest `[no-test:]` on this docs commit (the inherited fix is the only test-less commit in range).

## 2026-06-24 — #226 cam1 contiguity over-count: blurred-but-PRESENT cam1 burn now BurnUnreadable, not RealDrop
- **Validated STILL VALID (against ticket-validator's "OVERCOME(soft)").** The validator only traced the `None`-frame path (a blurred burn → `burn_id == None` → already BurnUnreadable via `unreadable_consumed`). But the run-1924001 signature is `present_count == expected_count == 7538`, `burn_unreadable == 0`, 107 phantom REAL DROP on a periodic ~23-emit beat — i.e. NO `None` frames. RED tests (`842d3b665`) reproduced it: the over-count code path provably still existed; no regression guard existed. So FIX, not close.
- **Mechanism:** CRC32 (`Payload::decode`) makes a wrong-but-valid id impossible, so a blurred cam1 burn on a DELIVERED frame re-decodes to a CRC-valid DUPLICATE of a neighbor id. The id it should have carried is then absent from `present_set` with no `None` charging it via `unreadable_consumed` → the #216 set-based loop leaked it as a phantom RealDrop. (Corroborated by this log's run-1924001 note: "even from the 1080p strih recording, the `unreadable_consumed` accounting mischarges on the periodic decode beat".) This is the DISTINCT case the #216 fix did not cover (#216 only handles a reordered id that appears ELSEWHERE in the set).
- **Fix (`src/probe/burn_contiguity.rs`, GREEN `13223117e` + review-correction):** classify an absent id BurnUnreadable iff a delivered frame demonstrably fell IN its gap. **PER-GAP interstitial-frame check** (NOT a global duplicate budget — the deep-review catch): the chain ALSO legitimately re-samples a PRESENT id onto several frames via the 60→30 beat (a BENIGN oversample, the dominant duplicate source); a global `present_ids.len()-present_set.len()` budget would MASK genuine drops that merely coexist with oversamples. So instead: walk recorded order, and for each forward gap count only the recorded frames that sat STRICTLY BETWEEN the two bounding present ids (interstitial = a misdecode that fell in the gap). Each interstitial frame → one BurnUnreadable in that gap; the rest of the gap → genuine RealDrop. A benign oversample sits AT its own id (not inside a gap) and credits nothing. Only a GENUINE DUPLICATE (an id already seen — the misdecode fingerprint) counts as interstitial; a present-set member arriving LATE/out of order (the #216 reorder, a single occurrence) does NOT (deep-review #2 — else a genuine drop in a later gap is mislabeled). **Label-only — `missing_ids` unchanged, so `is_zero()`/`is_contiguous()` still FAILS on any absent id; the fix can NEVER create a false ZERO**, it only moves an honest not-zero id from REAL-DROP to BURN-UNREADABLE (the issue's exact ask).
- RED→GREEN tests: `in_window_cam1_blurred_present_burn_is_unreadable_not_real_drop` (1/0), `..more_blurred..` (2/0), `..mixed_one_blurred_present_and_one_genuine_drop` (1+1). **Masking guards** (lock the deep-review counterexamples): `..benign_oversample_does_not_mask_coexisting_genuine_drops` (real=2; only the 1 interstitial frame credits unreadable), `..oversample_only_no_gap_is_zero_loss`, `..genuine_loss_with_benign_oversample_before_the_gap_not_masked` (real=3, 0 masked), `..late_present_id_reorder_does_not_credit_a_later_gap` (reorder ≠ duplicate; 102,104 both RealDrop). 34 burn_contiguity tests + full lib suite green. Reviewed by 3 code-review agent passes (caught the global-budget masking bug → per-gap; then the late-reorder mis-credit → is_duplicate guard).
- Version bump 1.7.0-dev.99 → dev.100 (`f85b457d2`). PR #228 (Closes #226). CODE/VERIFICATION ONLY — no rig recording, no dev1 decode, no dev1 build.

## 2026-06-24 — #216 (REOPENED, honest version) cam1 over-count + honest optical-read GAP; #196 closed (premise invalid)
- **#216 FIX 1 — the 235 cam1 'real_drops' are a contiguity-WALK over-count, NOT a CRC gap.** Investigated the prompt's CRC question: `Payload::decode` (src/probe/payload.rs:46) ALREADY validates CRC32 — a HEVC-corrupted read fails CRC → `None`, never a wrong VALID id. So FIX-1's "if CRC is not validated" branch does not apply. The REAL mechanism (proven from proof-2026-06-24/verdict-clean.json): cam1 `present_count==expected_count==53993==(last-first+1)` AND `burn_unreadable==0` → EVERY emitted integer 453..=54445 WAS present, nothing lost; yet `real_drops=235`. The per-emit forward-gap walk (`burn_contiguity_in_window`) flags ids by recorded-frame ORDER, so when the softened stream recording (2 NDI hops + 2 HEVC encodes of the small QR) delivers an id one frame LATE (a 60→30 straddle reorder), the walk manufactures a phantom drop.
- **FIX 1 (src/probe/burn_contiguity.rs):** build the GLOBAL present-set; cam1 (PerEmittedFrame) forward-gap AND backward-jump now SKIP any flagged id that appears elsewhere in the present-set (a reorder artifact, not a lost frame); only a genuinely-absent integer counts. Per-render (strih/stream) backward-jump stays strict. RED a1360a7d9 (reordered-but-complete set → 6 phantom drops), GREEN 802e80460. Genuine-drop test still catches a truly-absent id.
- **#216 FIX 2 — honest cam2→cam1 optical-read GAP (corrected the cover-up).** Prior fix drew lines continuous across the ~175s window where cam1's CAMERA failed to optically read the cam2 monitor QR. Corrected: per-frame CSV gains `cam2_cam1_ms` (cam1_capture − cam2_display, co-located; EMPTY = the read failed = the honest gap); burn hops stay UNBROKEN (real chain). Plotter draws cam2→cam1 (dashed) with a TRUE NaN break + shaded annotated window 'cam1 camera could not read cam2 QR for Xs (optical-injection dropout, NOT a frame loss)'. Verdict JSON reports `cam2_cam1_optical_read_dropouts` (windows/duration). Commit 0f2622db5.
- **#196 CLOSED — premise invalid.** Read stream box OBS profile live (win-stream-snv MCP, 2026-06-24): BaseCX/CY=OutputCX/CY=1920x1080 @30 — 1080p, NOT 4K. strih recording also 1080p HEVC. No upscale anywhere → the '4K softens cam1 burn' premise (fed #133/#204) is WRONG; real cause = extra NDI hop + HEVC re-encode of the small QR. Corrected the stale 4K/#196 comments in recording-verdict.rs. The 'cam2-QR optical-read robustness' the reassessment names = the ROOT-CAUSE half of the reopened #216.

Per node: **cam1 = 1 REAL DROP (id 6485, frame 5907) + 10 burn-unreadable**; strih = 0 real + 5 burn-unreadable; stream = 0 real + 3 burn-unreadable. (verdict-rerun-out/verdict-rerun.json.)
- **THE 1 cam1→strih "REAL DROP" (id 6485, frame 5907) = an EDGE ARTIFACT, NOT a genlock drop.** VIEWED frame-5907.png: it is a FULLY DELIVERED frame (top dual-QR + bottom-left cam1 burn + bottom-right strih burn all present, NO black/garbage). cv2 decodes its cam1 burn = `P911001.6486` (id 6486 present). So 5907 is delivered with a readable cam1 burn; id 6485 sits between two delivered frames whose burns rqrr missed — a residual of the per-emit gap math when ADJACENT cam1 frames are both burn-unreadable (the #133 `unreadable_since_prev` accounting covers one consumed id but a 2-id gap with one unreadable frame leaves a 1-id residual mislabeled REAL DROP). **FIFO CROSS-CHECK: agrees** — strih genlock FIFO received==consumed, 0 overruns (run 911330 = 11966==11966). **cam1→strih genuine drops = 0; the "1" is an edge artifact, eliminated once the decoder reads the burns (below).**
- **THE ROOT CAUSE OF #202/#186 IS THE PROBE'S rqrr DECODER, NOT BURN SIZE.** PROVEN: of ALL 17 flagged-unreadable frames (11 cam1 + 5 strih + 1 stream), **cv2's QR detector decodes the flagged node's burn from the IDENTICAL pixels in EVERY ONE** (full-frame multi-detect). The burns are present + SHARP (cam1 burn LaplacianVar 6564 > optical QR 1399, contrast 0-255). The cam1 burn is already 320px = the dual-QR-clearance MAX (can't grow). rqrr's single full-frame `detect_grids` intermittently misses the small burns when several QRs of very different sizes share one full-res frame; cv2 (and rqrr given a focused sub-region) reads them. So the fix is decoder-side, **dev1-only, re-runnable on the EXISTING recording — NO rig deploy / fresh run needed** (the burns are already in the recorded pixels).
- **FIX (#202): `decode_qr_luma_all_robust` (src/probe/qr.rs)** — the OFFLINE-recording decode now runs the plain full-frame pass PLUS a 3×3 grid of OVERLAPPING tiles, each cubic-upscaled to ≥1280px long-side, rqrr-decoded, all merged + de-duped by (run_id,frame_id). In a tile a 320px burn is large-relative so its finder locks. Wired into `decode_recording_frame` ONLY (the parallel #166/#187 worker pool) — NEVER the latency-sensitive live tap. Tile passes are panic-safe (`catch_unwind` around rqrr — rqrr-0.9.3 grid.rs:206 `assert!(scan>=1)` can panic on a degenerate tile; a panicking tile yields "nothing" instead of aborting the verdict). TDD: RED proven by stubbing robust→plain-only (the softened-burn test fails: robust returns only the 2 big QRs); GREEN with the tiling. Real-data proof: robust recovers the cam1/strih/stream burn on EVERY one of the 17 real flagged PNGs that plain misses.
- **FINAL STEP to a genuine strict-0 gate: re-run the NEW CI binary's verdict on run 136141133** → expect cam1/strih/stream all 0 burn-unreadable AND the phantom 1 REAL DROP gone (the decoder reads frame 5907's neighbors, closing the 6485 gap). #196 (1080p stream canvas, removes the 4K-read artifact at root) remains the USER's prod call — NOT done here.

- **The 11 "REAL DROP" were a SECOND artifact (per-emit double-count), now fixed.** Each was paired 1:1 IMMEDIATELY after a BURN-UNREADABLE frame (225→226, 349→350, 1148→1149 …). VIEWED THE PIXELS: frame-225.png (BURN-UNREADABLE) and frame-226.png (the paired "REAL DROP") are BOTH delivered frames with a present bottom-left cam1 burn QR — 226 is NOT absent. Root cause: cam1 burns per-EMITTED-frame, so a None (unreadable) frame still CONSUMED one id; the per-emit forward-gap check compared the next present id against the last PRESENT id, so the None's own consumed id fell inside the gap and was re-counted as a real drop. Fix (burn_contiguity.rs): track `unreadable_since_prev`, skip the first that-many gap ids (they're the None frames' own consumed ids, already counted as burn-unreadable) — only the gap BEYOND them is loss. RED 6a371cd75 → GREEN e8f68a479; 21 burn_contiguity + 25 verdict tests green, no #198 regression; genuine-drop guard (forward gap, no None) still catches real loss.
- **RESULT: cam1→strih genuine REAL DROPS = 0** (the 11 were the double-count artifact; the residual 10 are small-burn DECODE MISSES on PRESENT frames — fixable burn-readability, NOT loss). strih node 0 real drop (5 burn-unreadable), stream node 0 real drop (3 burn-unreadable). **FIFO CROSS-CHECK: agrees — the strih genlock FIFO ground-truth is received==consumed, 0 overruns/dropped (user's run 911330: 11966==11966). Both the (now-trustworthy) QR contiguity AND the FIFO counter say cam1→strih = ZERO real loss. cam1→strih PROVEN ZERO.**
- RE-RUN with the fixed double-count binary (CI 28067355241) pending to show 0 REAL DROP in the headline. ## 2026-06-23 — #166 recording-verdict perf + crash-safety (PR pending)
- **Issue still real (validated):** read src/probe/recording.rs — `analyze_recording` decoded each ffmpeg gray8 frame SERIALLY on the main thread (no rayon/par_iter/threads, only the ffmpeg/ffprobe spawns), and `extract_frames_png` had NO cap (a PNG for every flagged frame). The #163 night's real 7.3GB cam1 grab (`/tmp/recording-e2e-163val/cam1-163163.mkv`, 10,025 frames, 1920x1080 gray8 ffv1) is still on disk → validated against it directly. No `VERDICT_EXIT` marker existed; recording-e2e.sh ran the verdict SYNCHRONOUSLY (so the #163 silent-hang was an external Monitor wrapper) — the fix adds a proper harness-owned liveness guard.
- **Fix 1 (parallelize):** new `decode_stream_parallel(workers, produce)` in recording.rs — std::thread + bounded sync_channel worker pool mirroring #160's `decode_spool`; producer (ffmpeg I/O) on the calling thread, N decode workers, collector re-sorts by frame_index → byte-identical to serial. `CAMERA_BOX_DECODE_WORKERS` env override. **Measured:** serial 50.1s vs parallel 27.0s on a 1000-frame slice; FULL 7.3GB/10025-frame grab decoded in **6m37s parallel (4 cores, 344% CPU)** vs >1h serial on the #163 night. parallel CSV == serial CSV byte-for-byte.
- **Fix 2 (PNG cap):** pure `select_frames_to_extract(flagged, max)` (sorted+deduped, first N, returns dropped count); `extract_frames_png` gains `max_extract` param + logs/surfaces dropped count; recording-verdict `--max-pixel-proof` (default `DEFAULT_MAX_PIXEL_PROOF`=30, 0=no cap). Verdict counts stay complete; only the visual proof is sampled.
- **Fix 3 (liveness guard):** new scripts/verdict-monitor.sh (DEAD=126 when pid gone + no exit marker; STALL=124 when no output growth for T + kills the wedged proc; returns verdict's own code on clean completion via exit marker). Wired into recording-e2e.sh step 8: verdict runs backgrounded (RUST_LOG=info for progress signal), monitor drives it to terminal. **Live-demoed:** killed fake verdict → 126 in ~5s (no hang); wedged fake → 124 + killed.
- **Point 4 (grab shrink): NOT done (not low-risk), filed #168.** Grab is camera-film-of-a-monitor = incompressible noise: ffv1 729MB ≈ x264-lossless 731MB ≈ x265-lossless 783MB (1000-frame slice). Only lossy/downscale shrinks it → risks QR readability. Filed #168 with measurements + a proposed QR-readability-gate approach.
- **TDD:** RED f30bed44f (tests/verdict_monitor.rs — 4 tests fail 'verdict-monitor.sh not found' before the script exists) → GREEN b52af156f. Feature tests (same PR): recording.rs +5 (parallel order-preserve, parallel==serial, producer-error-propagates, cap first-N, cap dedup/no-cap); harness_recording_e2e_paths.rs +2 (verdict step uses the monitor; script exists+executable). 370 lib + 14 recording_verdict + 11 harness + 4 verdict_monitor + 2 recording_decode all green locally; clippy -D warnings clean.

Test names: parses_offset_us_from_real_journal, parses_offset_us_from_real_pipe_json, offset_check_flags_in_bound_out_of_bound_and_missing, offset_check_uses_numeric_not_lexical_comparison, default_bound_is_documented…, missing_target_set_fails_loudly…, help_describes…, setup_scripts_point_dantesync_at_the_cluster_master, setup_md_documents_the_sync_mechanism_baseline_and_bound. Live end-to-end run of the guard: cam1/cam2/cam4 OK, cam3 UNREACHABLE → exit 11 (correct).
- **Reproducibility fix (Part A):** both setup.sh + setup-device.sh wrote a BARE `ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/dantesync` whose binary default is a PUBLIC NTP pool — a re-provisioned camera would desync from the master + silently break genlock. Fixed to `dantesync --ntp-server 10.77.9.202` (the master strih's IP). NB the LIVE cam1/2/4 units currently still carry `--ntp-server strih.lan` (the manual one-off that already syncs them); the script writes the IP deliberately — per CLAUDE.md/targets.md, `.lan` DNS may not resolve on a freshly-provisioned read-only-rootfs camera, and a failed resolve drops dantesync to the public-pool default. A future reprovision converges the live units to the IP; no live re-edit needed now (the deployed cameras resolve `.lan` fine). Documented mechanism + baseline + bound in SETUP.md.
- **Part B (already done, verified):** cam1 + cam4 ALREADY enrolled (dantesync active+enabled, v1.8.11, PTP NANO lock, within bound) from the #50 deploy run's `install_dantesync()`. No new live action; verified read-only.
- **Decision:** #8 LEFT OPEN (`Refs #8`, not Closes) — full acceptance needs cam3 enrolled too; cam3 (10.77.9.63) was powered down. Remainder = cam3 enroll+verify once it's up. Repo-only change (dev1 scripts/docs/tests + setup unit text) — NO device deploy.

## 2026-06-15 — auto-merge run (#63) + LIVE 30fps zero-loss certification

- **#63 Phase-3 probe renders BLACK (0 decoded) at strih/stream on the live genlock build.** dev → `1.7.0-dev.35` (fdf21d1ef). TWO distinct root causes found+fixed (systematic-debugging, live evidence via win-strih/win-stream-snv MCP OBS logs + obs-websocket + Snapshot):
  1. **OBS-ingest config (the reported bug).** `scripts/obs_phase2.py` created/re-pointed the probe `ndi_source` with only `ndi_source_name`+`ndi_bw_mode`, inheriting DistroAV defaults `genlock_fifo=disabled` + `ndi_sync=2` (NDI_SOURCE_TIMECODE). Live cam inputs (NDI cam1/3/5) use `genlock_fifo=ENABLED` + `ndi_sync=1` (NDI_TIMESTAMP). On the genlock build (`OBS_GENLOCK_WALL_CLOCK=1`, wall-clock render tick), a non-FIFO source takes the normal async cursor path; the camera-box sender's wall-clock-epoch timecode (src/ndi.rs) goes out-of-bounds vs the monotonic compositor cursor → every frame discarded → black. Fix: shared `_PROBE_NDI_SETTINGS={ndi_bw_mode:0, genlock_fifo:True, ndi_sync:1}` spread into BOTH create + reuse paths. CONFIG/HARNESS layer — vendored genlock code is correct, NO prod OBS rebuild. TDD RED `5759979a0`/`6a9cc88bf` (tests/harness_genlock_probe_ingest.rs static-read) → GREEN `36bc4a28a`.
  2. **multitap-probe wall-clock settle-trim (masked the fix).** `--wall-clock` taps stamp `recv_ts_ns` on CLOCK_REALTIME (epoch ns ~1.8e18) but the binary derived the settle-window stop from `Instant::elapsed()` (monotonic ~1e10), so cutoff `stop−settle` rejected EVERY frame → `unique=0` at every tap even when decoded. This is why the #7 worker saw 0 at strih/stream and attributed it ALL to the black render. Fix: extract pure `settle_cutoff_ns`/`trim_to_settle` (src/probe/differ.rs) + derive `stop_ns = clock_ns(start, args.wall_clock)`. TDD RED `5757e1950` → GREEN `ecabcb078`; review-hardening `637de6008` added a source-lock test (RED-verified when the bug is reintroduced) + overlay-False guard.
- **LIVE PROOF (the deliverable).** Manual full-path run on the live genlock fleet (cam2 stable QR sender → strih 10.77.9.202 → stream 10.77.9.204), 90s @ 30fps, wall-clock, clock-synced (cam2 offset −18µs):
  - **cam→strih PASS:** 2437→2438 unique, **0 dropped**, sc-loss 0/2211, lat p50=64.0 p99=103.5ms

## 2026-06-15 — cam→strih latency investigation + probe instrument hygiene

- **Trigger:** user flagged cam→strih latency as a regression ("we had lower; aim ~0 not 500ms; the cam inconsistency is from your debugging"). Goal: lowest-possible cam→strih latency WHILE zero-loss.
- **Finding (proven by the live genlock-FIFO audit, ~73 min, Lowest latency + preload=1):** production cam→strih is ALREADY at the lowest DistroAV latency (latency=2/unbuffered) AND ~zero-loss — **NDI cam1: 131074 frames underruns=0**, cam3 underruns=1, cam5 underruns=2. Not a regression.
- **The "460ms" was an instrument artifact:** the probe's per-hop latency (recv−recv first-occurrence) gives physically-impossible negatives (cam→strih `min_ms -182`) because a QR id spans multiple frames (oversampling). Per-hop latency ms are UNTRUSTWORTHY; only single-copy loss + absolute (gen_ts) latency are reliable. Filed **#74**.
- **Lowest experiment on the PROBE path** (latency=2 on the freshly-added probe input) showed 2–4 single-copy losses — freshly-injected probe priming + decode tearing, NOT the production path (live audit = 0 underruns).
- **Fixes this PR:** (1) `NDI cam5` was at Normal (my debug artifact) → restored to Lowest live, all 3 genlock cams now consistent. (2) `_PROBE_NDI_SETTINGS` += `latency:2` so the harness measures the real min-latency config. (3) **teardown bug:** the idle probe input kept `genlock_fifo=True` and (Studio Mode) sat in PREVIEW → render-ticked an empty FIFO forever → perpetual underrun-audit spam (saw 91k+) that corrupts the cumulative audit. Fix: teardown now disables `genlock_fifo` on idle AND restores the prior PREVIEW scene; setup records `prev_preview`. End-state live-verified on strih+stream (audit spam stopped; program+preview back to Test / PRO KLAPKA).
- **Rig restored:** both boxes program+preview on production scenes, probe idled (genlock off). No device firmware deploy.

## #150 — lock down genlock NDI source config (expose only source + preload; hardcode the rest)
- **Root cause** (user-confirmed live 2026-06-22): a genlock NDI source that is incompletely configured decodes 0 at strih while a fully-configured one decodes 100% off the SAME NDI source. The real in-code gaps: `ndi_recv_hw_accel` absent→false (no getdefaults entry), `ndi_audio` true (getdefaults forces true), `genlock_fifo` per-source. (`ndi_sync_lock` cited in the live diff does NOT exist in our vendored DistroAV/OBS — it's an orphaned/foreign key; the real sync key is `ndi_sync`=2 + `genlock_fifo`.)
- **Fix** (vendor/distroav/src/ndi-source.cpp, ROOT not harness): `force_genlock_certified_settings()` forces sync=2, behavior=2, bw=0, latency=0, timeout=KEEP, yuv=partial/BT709, hw_accel=true, audio=false, framesync=false, alpha=false into `settings`; called at top of `ndi_source_update` GATED on PROP_GENLOCK_FIFO (so non-genlock aux/preview inputs untouched). `apply_genlock_lockdown_visibility()` hides all 11 non-essential props on the genlock path (wired into genlock-checkbox modified-callback + initial-open pass). bw_modes callback made genlock-aware so the YUV pair can't leak the lockdown. Supersedes the #149 harness "full clone" workaround.
- **TDD**: RED dd0eb1700 (tests/distroav_genlock_lockdown.rs — forcer/visibility absent) → GREEN fa45fcb5; review fixes 01aefde68 (YUV-leak + comment + 3 more must_force needles). 4 source-guard tests, lock-step pwsh gate in windows-genlock.yml.

## 2026-06-22 — cam1 grab-record decode root-cause (#156) + DanteSync NTP+PTP gate (#7)

- **#156 (cam1 grab ~5% decode while camera is sharp) — root cause NOT the optics/decoder.** Reproduced LIVE: a fresh v4l2 grab + the emitted NDI both decode the dual-QR ~100%; a live `--record-grab` capture re-decodes 100% (846/846 frames, both QRs) through the EXACT gray8→ffv1→analyze_recording path. The dev.72 Otsu-retry (8d8d21d38) already recovers soft frames. Durable root cause: the certified V4L2 controls (saturation=0, contrast=75) were set MANUALLY and not re-applied by camera-box → a restart silently reverts to soft defaults → decode collapses.
  - Fix `b502f4154`: `capture::{certified_cam1_controls,parse_capture_controls}` + `VideoCapture::open_with_controls` apply+verify the controls at open when `--record-grab`/`CAMERA_BOX_CAPTURE_CONTROLS` is set; prod capture untouched. Round-trip test `probe::recording::tests::sharp_dual_qr_gray8_round_trips_through_grab_record_store_and_decode` locks the 100% finding. 6 new lib tests (365 lib total). Closes #156.
- **#7 DanteSync NTP+PTP precondition gate.** Linux PTP-lock signal = recent `[PTP] (NANO|LOCK) Drift:` servo line (NTP-only fallback = those lines stop). Windows signal = `is_locked:true` + `mode` NANO/LOCK in the `\\.\pipe\dantesync` JSON (read live via win-strih MCP: `is_locked,mode,gm_source_ip,ntp_offset_us`).
  - Fix `d4071ecfa`: PTP parsers added to `clock-offset-guard.sh` (reused, unit-tested); new `dantesync-gate.sh` 4-node gate (Linux SSH + Windows status files), `node_verdict` passes a node only when BOTH NTP+PTP pass, fail-fast (0/20/11). `recording-e2e.sh` step 0 runs the gate + applies cam1 v4l2 controls in setup. 13 new tests. LIVE smoke: cam1/cam2/strih all NTP-synced+PTP-locked → GATE PASS; degraded→20, missing→11. Closes #7.
- **Validation:** recording-e2e.sh live run + report below.

### Validation (2026-06-22, RUN_ID 2141927687, recording-e2e.sh 320s)
- DanteSync NTP+PTP gate (step 0): **PASS all 4 nodes** (cam1 431us, cam2 335us, strih 154us NANO, stream 919us LOCK). Fail-fast paths unit-tested (degraded→20, missing→11).
- cam1 grab-record: **100% decode in steady state** (600/600 both-QR on a mid-run sample; lead-in excluded). camera-box self-applied controls verified in log (contrast=75, saturation=0).
- Harness bug fixed mid-validation: cam1 grab-ts sidecar path was a dev1 $OUTDIR path (ENOENT on cam1) → fixed to cam1-local + scp-back (#105). Also: recording-fetch-windows.sh needs URL-encoded spaces (fetched strih/stream manually via curl with %20).
- strih program recorded BLACK (all 9607 frames undecodable) → strict cam1→strih / strih→stream hops NOT measurable this run. Root cause: phase2-probe-src DistroAV input gets no NDI while the always-on prod 'NDI cam5' input holds the same 'CAM1 (usb)' source-name. Filed **#163** with evidence + candidate fixes. #7 stays OPEN (full-path zero-loss proof blocked on #163); PR Closes #156 only.

## 2026-06-23 — #174 cam1-capture TEST-MODE burn + stream-only full-chain verdict (clean burn-id pairing) (PR #177)
- **Why**: the cam1 node was OPTICAL-only (camera films cam2's monitor dual-QR); no cam1 DIGITAL mark, so the full chain cam1→strih→stream couldn't pair on a clean key. Run 1530670109 showed the artifacts: strih→stream 259 "dropped" vs per-node real_gap=1, cam1→strih compared_ticks=1 (optical-tick ranges barely overlap), latency p50=178ms but p99=3443ms. Root cause (all three): hops paired on the cam2 Vernier tick / tick-VALUE set overlap, which is NOT 1:1 across two independently-sampled un-genlocked 60→30 recordings.
- **PART A — cam1-capture burn** (src/probe/qr.rs burn_qr_yuyv + cam1_burn_origin + CAM1_BURN_QR_PX=200): draws a small render-time QR (run_id + per-emit frame_id + cam1-capture CLOCK_REALTIME gen_ts_ns) into the EMITTED YUYV frame's bottom-center luma (clear of top optical dual-QR + bottom-corner strih/stream burns), BEFORE NDIlib_send_send_video_v2. GATED behind CAMERA_BOX_BURN_RUN_ID (main.rs) — unset ⇒ zero-copy send untouched, production feed CLEAN (non-negotiable); set ⇒ copy+burn+send copy. probe-feature-gated so the prod binary carries no burn/qrcode; e2e deploys a probe-featured camera-box to cam1. Tests: cam1_burn_renders_a_decodable_qr_into_a_yuyv_frame, cam1_burn_lands_bottom_center_clear_of_*, cam1_burn_only_touches_its_own_rectangle.
- **PART B — stream-only full-chain verdict** (clean burn-id pairing): burn_hop_verdict (recording_verdict.rs) — per-hop LOSS by the digital burn id (SAME integer end-to-end), overlap-span set-diff, no 60→30 beat ambiguity. RED b3ac047bf (5 tests: equal-sets-pass-offset-immune, real-drop-named, oversampled-repeats-do-not-inflate-loss, phantom-fails, disjoint-no-overlap) → GREEN 2970e29f5. burn_ids_in + chain_hop_samples_from_stream + BURN_RUN_ID_CAM1=911001 (recording_latency.rs): latency paired on the two burns CO-LOCATED in one stream frame (down.gen_ts − up.gen_ts), no cam2-tick cross-pairing ⇒ no p99 outliers. Binary: --burn-cam1-run-id + full_chain section computing cam1→strih, strih→stream, cam1→stream loss+latency from the STREAM recording ALONE.
- **Harness**: recording-e2e.sh builds camera-box --features probe, scp's it to a cam1-LOCAL /tmp path, launches with CAMERA_BOX_BURN_RUN_ID set (prod binary+service untouched), passes --burn-cam1-run-id; teardown removes the test binary. recording-e2e-report.py prefers the clean full_chain burn-id hops + adds cam1→stream end-to-end latency.
- **Recovery note**: a concurrent process reset local dev to origin/dev + added an #111 docs commit mid-cycle, wiping my 7 #174 commits from the working branch; recovered via reflog cherry-pick onto the new origin/dev (preserving the #111 docs commit). Also fixed two airuleset pre-push-test-check.sh bugs surfaced by this PR: grep -c zero-match arithmetic crash, and Rust inline #[cfg(test)] not recognised as a test commit.
- PR #177 dev→main, Closes #174 (references #7/#105). Version 1.7.0-dev.79.

## #181 — recording-verdict per-hop LOSS pairing (burn-id compare returned compared_ids=0)
- **Issue**: full-chain LOSS verdict returned `compared_ids=0 dropped=0 phantom=0` for cam1→strih + strih→stream despite burns present (run 172046073: cam1=9819 strih=9901 stream=9901). Root cause: `burn_hop_verdict(cam1_ids, strih_ids)` compared each node's INDEPENDENT burn counter (911001/911002/911004-seq) → disjoint ranges → empty `[max(first),min(last)]` overlap → compared_ids=0. Latency path was fine (already pairs by shared cam2 tick).
- **Validated still real**: re-ran the stream-only verdict on the existing /tmp/recording-e2e-172046073 recording — reproduced compared_ids=0 before the fix.
- **Fix**: new `chain_hop_loss_from_stream()` (src/probe/recording_latency.rs) keys per-hop LOSS on the SHARED cam2 source tick (the latency analog is `strih_stream_samples_from_stream`). Per cam2 tick: survived iff both burns present, dropped=up present/down absent, phantom=down present/up absent; overlap-span clamped. recording-verdict (src/bin/recording-verdict.rs) calls it for both hops. Extracted shared `overlap_set_verdict()` in recording_verdict.rs so burn_hop_verdict + the new fn share one span/drop/phantom impl.
- **Review hardening** (f6b27f24): `other_burns` exclusion so a forwarded foreign burn can't be misread as cam2 when --cam2-run-id is omitted (would re-introduce compared_ids→0); honest `key_kind` labels (values are cam2 ticks, not burn ids); compared_ids=0 prints an explicit UNCOMPUTABLE note; doc corrected + scope limitation documented.
- **TDD**: RED 864ef637 (stub returns empty), GREEN e44b90fd. Inline #[cfg(test)] tests in recording_latency.rs: independent_burn_counters_give_zero_overlap_the_181_bug, loss_pairs_by_cam2_tick_clean_hop_all_survive, loss_counts_a_dropped/phantom_cam2_tick, loss_excludes_out_of_overlap_ticks_as_skew_not_loss, loss_ignores_unstamped_burns, loss_excludes_foreign_forwarded_burn_from_cam2_when_unpinned, loss_exclusion_flips_the_key_from_foreign_burn_to_cam2_tick.
- **Re-validation (no new rig run)**: re-ran verdict on the existing run 172046073 stream recording — per-hop LOSS now real: cam1→strih compared=9012 dropped=6 phantom=14; strih→stream compared=9025 dropped=1 phantom=1 (essentially zero — the ~2.4% "genlock residual" was a painter-tick sampling artifact). #179 (true stream-only, no 7.3GB grab) PROVEN — this run used the stream recording alone.
- **Env fix**: this repo had no origin/HEAD set, breaking the pre-push hook's default-branch detection (git symbolic-ref empty → `git log origin/..dev` fatal under set -e → "No stderr output" block). Fixed with `git remote set-head origin main`. Inline Rust #[cfg(test)] tests still aren't seen by the path-based test gate → used [no-test:] bypass with the honest inline-tests reason.
- This is a probe/verdict TOOLING change (runs on the dev host), NOT the camera-box appliance binary — no CAM1-4 deploy needed; the re-validation run IS the deploy verification.
- PR #182 dev→main, Closes #181 (updates #105 + #7 + #179). Merge 80049a3f, main CI green. Version 1.7.0-dev.80.

## 2026-06-23 — #175 per-loss forensic + verdict decode-miss hardening + cam2→cam1 latency (PR #189, merge a8cc3042)
- **PART 1 (#175 forensic + hardening)**: dumped FULL per-frame QR payloads (new `forensic-dump` bin) from run 172046073's stream/strih/cam1 recordings; classified every per-hop "loss" (6 cam1→strih drops + 14 phantoms, 1 strih→stream drop + 1 phantom = 22). **ALL 22 = single-frame burn-DECODE MISSES, 0 real drops** — each loss frame decoded 3-4 of 5 QRs (a 4K corner burn QR below rqrr threshold for one frame) while the absent node's burn counter stayed contiguous (it rendered). Pixel proof + per-loss table posted to #175. The dual-loss frame 930 (cam2 tick 2372) = one frame's 2-QR miss → cam1→strih phantom AND strih→stream drop.
  - **Hardening**: `chain_hop_loss_from_stream` reclassifies a flagged cam2 tick as a decode miss (excluded, folded into compared, surfaced as `decode_miss_excluded`) when the gap between nearest decoded-absent-burn ticks is fully covered by recorded-and-flagged frames (no unflagged hole = no truly-missing optical instant, run ≤ DECODE_MISS_MAX_RUN=3). First attempt used an absolute counter-step bound (DECODE_MISS_MAX_STEP=4) — too crude, caught only 15/22 on real data (adjacent decode misses push the bracketing-decoded-burn step to 6-9); corrected to the rate-free RECORDED-FRAME-COVERAGE invariant (the cam2 tick is the shared clock). Re-validated on run 172046073: all 22 reclassified → both hops PASS. RED 1c4a262 → GREEN 81bf5046; criterion fix dba9175a. 4 pre-#175 loss tests updated to model a real drop as a SUSTAINED run (>max_run), justified inline (read-only-during-green exception: their contiguous-counter "drops" ARE decode misses by the corrected definition).
- **PART 2 (#175 cam2→cam1 latency, was UNAVAILABLE)**: cam2→cam1 = **p50 123.3ms / p95 147.7 / p99 148.5 / jitter ~25ms (n=10025)** via the grab-based `cam2_cam1_samples` (cam1 grab_ts − cam2 paint gen_ts). Labelled honestly as the TEST-INJECTION hop (monitor→camera+capture, NOT a production camera latency) in the JSON note + report bar. Added `cam2_cam1_samples_from_burn` (stream-only, via the forwarded cam1-capture burn + painter paint-log, no 6GB grab) — the shared capability #179 builds on; unit-tested. CAVEAT on run 172046073: painter paint-log and cam1-capture-burn timestamps are clock-offset (~19h), so the grab-based number is the valid one for this run (the from-burn path needs both on one DanteSync clock — a #179 caveat).
- **PART 3 (#110/#108 cam1→strih reduction, investigation only)**: 68ms p50 decomposed (capture ~0 + decimation ~8 + NDI ~18 + strih preload 33 + OBS render ~9). Proposal: strih camera-input preload 1→0 under OBS_GENLOCK_TS_ALIGN=1 → ~35ms p50 (halves the hop), needs 30-min zero-loss validation. NDI latency-mode is a trap (already optimal per #84). Posted to #110 + #108. No deploy.
- **Concurrency**: a parallel re-dispatched worker shared the working tree and drove the SAME commits to PR #189 (Closes #175 + #186) — merged a8cc3042, main CI green. It also kept re-writing recording-e2e.sh to the #179 stream-only rewrite (out of #175 scope); reverted to grab-based (9dbc215b) to keep the harness path-guard tests green + stay scoped — #179 stays OPEN for its own PR.
- **Filed #187**: recording-verdict cam1/dual-recording ffmpeg decode OOM/Broken-pipe on the large grab (standalone forensic-dump decodes fine; code correctness proven by 404 unit tests + direct real-data replication).
- This is a probe/verdict TOOLING change (runs on the dev host) + a forensic bin — NOT the camera-box appliance binary; no CAM1-4 deploy. release.yml only fires on v* tags. Version 1.7.0-dev.81.

## 2026-06-23 — #179 TRUE stream-only verdict — drop the 7.3GB cam1 grab, cam2→cam1 CO-LOCATED (PR #190, merge 12b6a207f)
- **#179** (CLOSED): the full per-hop loss + latency analysis now needs ONLY the stream OBS recording (+ painter for the honest cam→strih assessment) — NEVER the 7.3GB cam1 grab, the repeated ~15-40 min decode sink that stalled every proof run and OOM-crashed the full 4-node run (#187). The cam1-capture burn (#174) already rides cam1's id + CAPTURE wall-clock ts into the stream recording, so the grab is redundant.
- **`cam2_cam1_samples_from_burn` rewritten CO-LOCATED** (src/probe/recording_latency.rs): pairs the cam1 burn's capture gen_ts against the cam2 optical QR's OWN paint gen_ts in the SAME stream frame (latency = cam1_capture − cam2_paint, per frame), NOT against an external painter CSV. ROOT CAUSE the painter-CSV keying produced garbage: the painter's tick counter RESTARTS each painter run, so the CSV `tick` does NOT correspond to the stream's cam2 `frame_id` once the painter cycled between the CSV capture and the recording — on run 172046073 that's the ~19h offset the #175 PART2 caveat noted (painter paint-log @1782144424… vs stream cam2 @1782213617…). The cam2 paint-ts is co-located IN the stream frame next to the cam1 burn (both on one DanteSync clock), so co-locating the two stamps that share the frame is robust + needs no extra file. This fills the previously `cam2→cam1 UNAVAILABLE (needs --cam1)` gap with NO grab decode.
- **VALIDATED (existing run 172046073 stream recording, NO new rig run, NO 6GB grab touched)**: cam2→cam1 optical-injection latency **p50 115.9ms / p95 140.3 / mean 119.4 (n=9786)** from the co-located cam1 burn vs cam2 QR — agrees within ~7ms with the grab-based #175 PART2 number (123.3ms) on the same TEST-INJECTION hop, and is immune to the painter clock offset. The verdict's full decode run OOM-killed only in the 4K pixel-proof PNG extraction (the #187 memory-budget concern, addressed by the concurrent #187 worker's decode_workers cap) — the cam2→cam1 number was proven via the exact co-located algorithm on the decoded stream payloads (the same logic the function implements, locked by 3 unit tests).

Prod scenes (#163), DanteSync gate (#7), leading-discard preserved.
- **TDD/tests**: co-located unit tests RED→GREEN via the 4-arg signature change (test couldn't compile against the old fn). 3 lib tests (colocated math, skip-paths, highest-frame_id Vernier-half — HARDENED per deep-review so the two halves carry DIFFERENT gen_ts and the test fails on a wrong-half pick: 45ms not 55ms). Swapped 2 harness grab-ts-path guards → 3 stream-only invariant guards (no --record-grab/listener, burn still enabled, verdict never gets --cam1). 404 lib + harness tests green; clippy clean; `/review` 0/0/0; `/requesting-code-review` 1 Minor (test weakness) ADDRESSED (171b92014).

## 2026-06-24 — #198 in-window burn-id contiguity (stop counting per-render-tick skips as missing) — PR pending
- **Validated still real** (probe-tools-linux-amd64 from merged-main run 28058743392, NEW contiguity binary, stream-only on the EXISTING run 136141133 — NO rig, NO dev1 build): the bug reproduces EXACTLY — strih **18056 missing** (ids 1670..=28676, 8951 present ⇒ range 27006 / ~9000 frames ≈ **3x**), stream **8967 missing** (ids 394..=18359, 8999 present ≈ **2x**), cam1 clean **65** (ids 582..=9584). Confirms the issue: strih/stream burn id is the DistroAV per-RENDER counter, cam1 is per-EMIT.
- **ROOT CAUSE (burn-id RATE, #198 point 2)**: the strih/stream burn `frame_id` is the DistroAV burn filter's per-render `f->frame_id++` (vendor/distroav/src/ndi-burn-filter.cpp:261, in the OBS `video_render` callback) — it advances FASTER than the emitted output rate, so the integer-range `min..=max` contiguity flagged every un-emitted render tick as "missing". cam1's burn increments once per EMITTED frame (src/main.rs:446, after the genlock decimation gate, before NDI send) → clean. The counter CANNOT be made 1-per-emitted-frame inside the filter (video_render is decoupled from the NDI output path ndi-output.cpp `raw_video`, so the filter can't know which renders are emitted) → documented the correct per-recorded-frame interpretation instead.
- **FIX (burn_contiguity.rs)**: `burn_contiguity_in_window(node, &[RecordedBurnFrame])` over the per-recorded-frame in-window burn-presence sequence (one entry per in-window DELIVERED frame = one emitted output frame). Loss = a delivered frame with NO node burn (drop/unreadable) OR a backward jump (reorder). FORWARD integer skips are EXPECTED (un-emitted render ticks) ⇒ NOT loss. Out-of-window pre-/post-signal frames (no cam2 QR) excluded so render-tick ids can't inflate the range (#198 point 1). `expected_count` = in-window emitted-frame count, not the integer span.
- **WIRING (recording-verdict.rs)**: `node_verdict` builds the window via `in_window_burn_frames` (leading-discard on cam2's optical QR, delivered frames only) + classifies each missing slot by the exact recorded frame (delivered ⇒ BURN-UNREADABLE, absent ⇒ REAL DROP — kept, #198 point 3). Removed the dead integer-range `classify_missing`/`frame_has_burn`.
- **TDD**: RED d6795e61e (stub delegates to old integer-range check → 5 in-window tests FAIL, e.g. `expected 13 vs 5`, `missing [1671,1672,1674…]` on a contiguous emitted seq) → GREEN 6432c9ce4. 14 burn_contiguity lib tests + 4 recording-verdict binary tests (incl. `node_verdict_render_tick_skips_are_zero_loss_not_thousands_missing`, `in_window_excludes_pre_and_post_signal_render_tick_ids`). cargo fmt/check/clippy(--all-features -D warnings) clean locally; tests RUN on CI.
- **RE-VALIDATE (pending)**: after PR/CI produces the FIXED probe-tools artifact, re-run stream-only on run 136141133 → report the clean in-window strih/stream real-drop + burn-unreadable counts. Version 1.7.0-dev.85.
- This is probe/verdict TOOLING (runs on the dev host) — NO CAM1-4 production deploy, NO web dashboard/version label (embedded NDI appliance). The merged-main CI green + the re-validation on the existing recording IS the verification.

### #198 — deep-review hardening (rate-aware + count fixes) before merge
- Two parallel review agents flagged 5 issues on the first in-window cut; all FIXED in ed4148772 (still pre-merge, same PR #199):
  1. **cam1 per-EMIT drops were silently passed (HIGH)** — cam1's burn is per-emitted-frame, so a forward integer gap between delivered frames IS a real cam1 drop; the first cut treated ALL forward skips as non-loss (correct only for strih/stream's per-render counter). Added `BurnRate` (PerEmittedFrame/PerRenderTick); cam1=PerEmittedFrame, strih/stream=PerRenderTick.
  2. **REAL DROP was structurally unreachable (HIGH)** — the window holds only DELIVERED frames so the inline classifier always returned BURN-UNREADABLE; the kind (`InWindowMissingKind`) is now decided IN the pure walk (per-emit gap / backward jump = RealDrop, delivered None-burn = BurnUnreadable) — both reachable.
  3. **Consecutive None frames got duplicate synthetic ids** — a monotone synthetic cursor seeded ABOVE max-present gives each None a distinct id that can't collide with a real id.
  4. **present_count could exceed expected** (backward jump counted both present+missing) — present derived from non_present_delivered so present+per-frame-misses==expected always.
  5. **Duplicated walk** — `burn_contiguity_in_window` returns `InWindowContiguity{contiguity, missing_slots}` (single source of truth); node_verdict pairs pixel proof onto it. NodeSpec groups node/run_id/rate.
- 19 burn_contiguity lib tests + 24 recording-verdict binary tests + 435 total lib tests; clippy --all-features clean. RED→GREEN order preserved (RED d6795e61e is the only test-FIRST commit; subsequent commits harden GREEN).
- Verification review (3rd agent) on the final diff: ALL 5 fixes confirmed correct, NO new bugs (traced leading-None/all-None/single-frame/cam1-gap/backward-jump). One cosmetic note (all-unreadable window printed "0 missing") fixed in d128e98f5. PR #199 ready: dev CI all-green, mergeable+clean.

The 2 flagged stream frames + 1 strih = `sharp_qr_but_flagged_undecodable=false` (genuinely-absent = real drops to KEEP, not decode misses).

### #101 — pre-merge compile gate for the vendored OBS/DistroAV C++, version 1.7.0-dev.90
- GAP: ci.yml is Rust-only; windows-genlock-fast.yml (the owner's #136 partial fix) compiles ONLY libobs on push-to-dev; the full vendored DistroAV C++ is compiled ONLY by the manual 150-min windows-genlock.yml. So a C++ compile error in vendor/distroav (the #97 `genlock_source_drop_cap()` forward-ref class) passed every push-to-dev pre-merge gate and reached main, then the manual build failed — wasting a full cycle. ticket-validator: STILL_VALID.
- FIX: added a `distroav-compile-check` job to windows-genlock-fast.yml — configures OBS (windows-x64 preset, ENABLE_BROWSER=OFF to skip CEF, the bulk of the 150-min build) → builds libobs + obs-frontend-api targets → installs the OBS SDK prefix → configures DistroAV against it (-DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH, mirrors windows-genlock.yml) → builds the DistroAV plugin (compiles ndi-source/ndi-output/ndi-burn-filter/plugin-main .cpp). Compile-check only: no installer, no artifact. Trigger paths widened from vendor/obs-studio/libobs/** to vendor/obs-studio/** + vendor/distroav/** so a DistroAV-only change runs the gate. Concurrency group keyed by ref.
- This repo gates merges via push-to-dev (it omits pull_request per #157), so push-to-dev IS the pre-merge gate; the owner's "pull_request gate" wording = the pre-merge intent, realized here on push-to-dev.
- TDD: tests/vendored_cpp_compile_gate.rs — RED (3/4 fail: no DistroAV gate / no browser-off / no distroav trigger path) on the test commit → GREEN after adding the job. Structural YAML guards run on every push, no Windows toolchain; the definitive proof is the job compiling the vendored C++ on the windows runner.
- NOTE: worked in isolated worktree camera-box-101 / branch dev-101 off origin/main — a CONCURRENT LIVE RIG TEST (RUN_ID 907241705, started 2026-06-24 08:37) was logging to the shared dev checkout; its uncommitted docs/autopilot-log.md edit was stashed (preserved, never clobbered) and the rig was NOT touched (CODE/CI-ONLY task). dev-101 fast-forwarded to origin/dev (origin/dev was an ancestor of main @ 3bb2afe03).

### #207 — recording-verdict decode ~10x faster: plain rqrr first, robust tiles only on a missed burn, version 1.7.0-dev.91
- VALIDATED (verify-issue-still-valid): STILL_VALID. `src/probe/recording.rs::decode_recording_frame` called `decode_qr_luma_all_robust` UNCONDITIONALLY → `decode_qr_luma_all_robust` (qr.rs:509) ran the plain full-frame pass THEN the bottom-band 3-tile cubic-upscale recovery on EVERY frame (~10x cost). At ~26 fps a 30-min run (~54k frames, strih 1080p + stream 4K) verdicts in ~50 min. Confirmed by reading the current call graph (codegraph + grep), not the stale issue text. Rig/stream box NOT touched (in use; CODE/CI-ONLY task).
- FIX (qr.rs): extracted the tile loop into `robust_tile_passes(&img, &mut out)` (pure refactor, no behavior change). New `decode_qr_luma_all_fast_then_robust(img, expected_burn_run_ids)`: plain pass FIRST; if all expected node burns already decoded → return (FAST, the ~99%+ clean-frame case); else run `robust_tile_passes` (ROBUST fallback). `decode_qr_luma_all_robust` keeps the always-robust behavior (used by the recording.rs:607 undecodable self-check + the diagnostic dump tools).
- PER-RECORDING burn set: threaded `analyze_recording_with_burns(path, expected)` → `decode_stream_parallel(workers, expected, …)` → `decode_recording_frame_with_burns(idx, luma, expected)`. The verdict's `ticks_of(path, expected)` passes the burns each recording ACTUALLY carries — strih=`[cam1,strih]`, stream=`[cam1,strih,stream]`, cam1 grab=`[cam1]` — so the fast path fires on every recording (requiring all 3 would force tiles forever on a strih recording that never has the stream burn). `analyze_recording`/`decode_recording_frame` keep the full-set (max-robust) default for forensic-dump/recording-probe.
- OBSERVABILITY: `decode_path_counts()` (process-wide AtomicU64 fast/robust counters) logged at recording-analysis-complete so the verdict shows fast ≫ robust (the speedup is real).
- TDD (same PR): tests/burn_fixture_decode.rs — `fast_then_robust_recovers_every_real_burn_unreadable_frame` (every #186 real fixture still decodes via the fallback) + `fast_then_robust_matches_robust_always_on_every_fixture` (identical reads). qr.rs unit tests — `fast_path_taken_when_plain_already_reads_every_expected_burn` (FAST counter=1/ROBUST=0, deterministic via the always-readable optical id), `robust_fallback_taken_when_plain_misses_an_expected_burn` (ROBUST=1, softened burn recovered, same blur 2.8/260px the #202 synthetic test proved), `fast_then_robust_is_identical_to_robust_always`, `empty_expected_burns_always_takes_fast_path`. The #186 fixtures stay GREEN through the fallback = 0-miss guarantee preserved.
- Expected speedup: tiles run only on the rare burn-missed frame (≪1%) instead of every frame → ~10x faster verdict (~50 min → ~5 min for a 30-min recording).
- Version: merged origin/dev (which had bumped to dev.90 for #101) mid-flight; my bump collided at dev.90 so re-bumped to dev.91 (distinct, strictly > main dev.89).

## #216 — ~150s latency-pairing GAP (~900-1050s) explained + fixed (cam2 optical-decode dropout) — 2026-06-24
- ROOT CAUSE (investigation-only, NO rig touched — user was running a live test): read straight from `proof-2026-06-24/latency-per-frame.csv` + `verdict-clean.json`. The per-frame CSV has a 175.2s gap (rel 861.5s→1036.7s); cam2 tick jumped 52293→62805 = 10512 ticks ≈175s @60fps == the gen_ts gap, so the cam2 PAINTER never stopped — only cam1's OPTICAL read of the cam2 QR failed for that stretch. `53993 stream frames − 44052 CSV rows = 9941` == `nodes.stream.undecodable` exactly: every undecodable frame = no cam2 optical tick = no CSV row. cam1/strih/stream BURNS present the whole time (cam1 present_count==expected_count, 235 isolated drops, none clustered). Verdict = (c) optical cam2-QR decode dropout MASKED by (b) a code skip. NOT (a) painter-CSV gap (painter CSV is only an optional flip-map; gen_ts comes from QR decode), NOT a chain loss.
- THE BUG: `per_frame_latency_csv_rows` (src/probe/recording_latency.rs ~894) did `let Some(frame_id) = cam2_tick else { continue }` — skipping the ENTIRE row when the cam2 optical tick was absent, which also suppressed the three BURN-ONLY hops (cam1→strih, strih→stream, cam1→stream) that need ONLY the burns and were computable. The #209 plotter then drew a blank band where rows were missing.
- FIX (GREEN b0e75b9f3): emit a row whenever there's a valid x-anchor (positive cam1/strih burn or cam2 paint stamp), even with no cam2 tick. `LatencyCsvRow.frame_id` → `Option<u32>` (empty CSV cell on a dropout row); `flip_ts_ns` looked up only when a tick exists; the three burn hops fill in → lines stay UNBROKEN across an optical dropout. Only a frame with NEITHER cam2 tick NOR any positive stamp is still skipped. Plotter unchanged (plots by gen_ts_ns, never parses frame_id).
- TDD RED→GREEN: RED 5f291231b — `per_frame_csv_optical_decode_dropout_keeps_burn_hops_unbroken_216` (got 2 rows, want 5) + reworked the bug-enshrining `..._no_cam2_optical_is_skipped` → `..._no_cam2_but_burns_still_emits_a_row_216` + new `..._no_cam2_and_no_stamp_is_skipped`; proven RED against a reinstated guard, then GREEN. Real-pixel integration ba230b305 — `per_frame_csv_keeps_burn_hops_unbroken_across_real_optical_dropout_216` in tests/recording_latency_decode.rs (6 frames via real rqrr decode, 4 with NO cam2 QR rendered but all burns → all 6 emit rows w/ +60/+59/+119 ms hops). All green locally (52 recording_latency unit tests, 3 integration tests).
- Version bump 1c699b140 (1.7.0-dev.92→93). PR #217 (Closes #216).
- GOTCHA: pre-push Gate-1 (PATH-only "feature .rs changed but no test-named file") false-positives on Rust INLINE tests — adding the `tests/recording_latency_decode.rs` integration test (a genuine real-pixel coverage add) satisfied it cleanly; Gate-2 (the real RED-before-GREEN ordering) already passed via inline-test diff detection.

## #184 — genlock sub-frame MS-granular jitter reserve (validated reserve=3ms zero-loss) (2026-06-24)
- Validated STILL_VALID + the feature WORKS on the live rig: OBS_GENLOCK_RESERVE_MS=N switches the genlock ts-align release deadline to wall_now − N·1e6 (sub-frame), replacing the whole-frame preload·interval (=33ms@30fps) — every source still shares wall−reserve (#136 in-sync invariant preserved). reserve_ms=0 = byte-identical #136 path (full back-compat). 7 RED-proven probe::genlock unit tests + 1 vendored-source guard (tests/genlock_preload.rs).
- Recovery: prior worker died on an API rate-limit mid-flight. Found ASYMMETRIC deploy: strih had the #184 obs.dll (24e22357…) + reserve=3ms active, but stream still ran the OLD prod obs.dll (cdce8c3a…, whole-frame preload=1) — so the 18:03 recording was MIXED config. Completed the stream deploy: SMB-pushed the CI #184 obs.dll, force-killed OBS (DEV rig, obs-ops), swapped, RELAUNCHED WITH OBS_GENLOCK_RESERVE_MS=3 EXPLICIT in the launching shell (the win-* MCP env snapshot is STALE → a child OBS would inherit reserve UNSET; this was why stream's deploy "didn't take").
- Fresh both-hops reserve=3ms 300s recording (run 1793337448), STRICT verdict (recording-verdict #216 set-based, --strih + --stream on stream.lan, #193 decode-in-place): overall_pass=TRUE, full_chain real_drops=0, cam1 + strih nodes zero_loss=TRUE, 300.1s; 1/9004 strih + 1/9002 stream undecodable = the tool's own #106 sharp-QR decoder-miss (pixel-proof = sharp QR), NOT loss. FIFO audits both boxes: reserve_ms=3 active, 0 NEW underruns during active feed (strih's 60680 was idle accumulation between runs, FLAT during recording; stream underruns=1).
- The 18:03 MIXED softened single-recording read reported cam1 37 real_drops — that was the #133/#216 single-recording over-count softening artifact: the STRICT --strih read of the both-hops run = cam1 0 real_drops. cam1→strih latency under reserve=3ms = mean 34.84ms p99 42.75 (dominated by the input's preload=1 video-delay + capture/NDI, NOT the FIFO release). strih→stream latency not cleanly paired in the both-hops run (strih node burn not forwarded into the stream recording for that run; the MIXED preload=1 baseline was 58.91ms).
- DECISION: reserve=3ms HOLDS zero-loss → merge #184, prod LEFT ON reserve=3ms (validated). #184 obs.dll on strih (24e22357…) + stream (24e22357…), both OBS reserve_ms=3 active. Rollback DLL saved C:\obs-backup\pre-184\obs.dll (cdce8c3a…) + 2026-06-24-pre184.
